## 13.1 可观测性测试概述 （来自：第4篇-第13章-大数据系统可观测性与监控测试【进阶】）

随着大数据系统规模的扩大和复杂度的增加，传统的监控方式已无法满足系统运维和故障排查的需求。可观测性（Observability）作为一种新兴的系统监控理念，通过整合分布式追踪、指标监控和日志分析三大支柱，为系统提供全面的可视性和问题诊断能力，是确保大数据系统稳定性和可靠性的关键技术。在大数据环境下，可观测性不仅关乎技术层面的系统健康，更是业务连续性和数据质量的重要保障。

### 13.1.1 可观测性三大支柱 （来自：第4篇-第13章-大数据系统可观测性与监控测试【进阶】）

#### 分布式追踪 （来自：第4篇-第13章-大数据系统可观测性与监控测试【进阶】）

- **概念**：分布式追踪通过为每个请求生成唯一的追踪ID，并在服务间传递，构建完整的调用链路，帮助理解请求在分布式系统中的流转路径和性能瓶颈。在大数据环境中，分布式追踪尤为重要，因为数据处理通常跨越多个系统和阶段，涉及复杂的依赖关系。追踪可以跨越从数据采集、转换、分析到可视化的全过程，提供端到端的可观测性视图。

- **核心组件**：

  - **追踪器（Tracer）**：负责生成和记录Span数据，是应用程序与追踪系统交互的主要接口

  - **Span**：表示一个具体的操作或请求处理过程，包含时间戳、持续时间、标签、事件等信息，是分布式追踪的基本单位

  - **上下文传播**：确保追踪ID和相关上下文在服务间、进程间甚至跨语言环境中正确传递

  - **收集器（Collector）**：接收和处理来自各服务的追踪数据，进行聚合、处理和转发

  - **存储和查询**：持久化追踪数据并提供高效的查询接口，支持复杂的筛选和分析操作

- **主流工具**：OpenTelemetry、Jaeger、Zipkin、SkyWalking

- **应用场景**：性能瓶颈定位、服务依赖分析、故障根因追溯、资源使用优化、异常模式识别

**大数据环境中的分布式追踪实现示例**：

```python
# 大数据处理流水线中的OpenTelemetry追踪实现 - 增强版
import time
import random
from opentelemetry import trace, metrics
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace import TracerProvider
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import BatchSpanProcessor, ConsoleSpanExporter
from opentelemetry.exporter.jaeger.thrift import JaegerExporter
from opentelemetry.sdk.resources import SERVICE_NAME, Resource, PROCESS_PID, DEPLOYMENT_ENVIRONMENT
from opentelemetry.context import attach, detach
from opentelemetry.propagate import inject, extract
from opentelemetry.trace import Status, StatusCode

# 配置全局追踪器
resource = Resource(attributes={
    SERVICE_NAME: "bigdata-processing-pipeline",
    DEPLOYMENT_ENVIRONMENT: "production",
    "cluster.name": "data-lake-cluster",
    "team.owner": "data-engineering"
})

# 创建追踪提供者
provider = TracerProvider(resource=resource)

# 配置多种导出器
jaeger_exporter = JaegerExporter(
    agent_host_name="jaeger-collector",
    agent_port=6831,
)

console_exporter = ConsoleSpanExporter()

# 添加批处理处理器（生产环境）
batch_processor = BatchSpanProcessor(jaeger_exporter)
provider.add_span_processor(batch_processor)

# 可选：添加控制台处理器（开发环境调试用）
# provider.add_span_processor(BatchSpanProcessor(console_exporter))

# 设置全局追踪提供者
trace.set_tracer_provider(provider)

class DataPipelineTracer:
    """大数据流水线专用追踪工具类"""

    @staticmethod
    def create_context():
        """创建新的追踪上下文"""
        context = {}
        inject(context)
        return context

    @staticmethod
    def extract_context(headers):
        """从headers中提取追踪上下文"""
        return extract(headers)

    @staticmethod
    def with_context(context, func, *args, **kwargs):
        """在指定上下文中执行函数"""
        token = attach(context)
        try:
            return func(*args, **kwargs)
        finally:
            detach(token)

def data_ingestion_stage(input_source, context=None):
    """数据采集阶段的高级追踪实现"""
    tracer = trace.get_tracer("data.ingestion")

    # 定义子操作的追踪辅助函数
    def _process_source_chunk(chunk_id, chunk_size):
        with tracer.start_as_current_span(f"process_chunk_{chunk_id}") as span:
            span.set_attribute("chunk.id", chunk_id)
            span.set_attribute("chunk.size", chunk_size)

            # 模拟处理延迟
            processing_time = random.uniform(0.1, 0.5)
            time.sleep(processing_time)

            # 模拟可能的错误
            if random.random() < 0.05:  # 5%概率失败
                span.set_status(Status(StatusCode.ERROR))
                span.add_event("chunk_processing_failed", {
                    "error_type": "timeout",
                    "retry_attempt": 1
                })
                raise Exception(f"处理块 {chunk_id} 超时")

            span.set_attribute("processing.time_ms", int(processing_time * 1000))
            span.add_event("chunk_processing_complete")
            return chunk_size

    try:
        if context:
            # 从外部上下文提取追踪信息
            token = attach(DataPipelineTracer.extract_context(context))

        with tracer.start_as_current_span("data_ingestion") as span:
            # 添加关键属性
            span.set_attribute("source.type", input_source["type"])
            span.set_attribute("source.location", input_source["location"])
            span.set_attribute("source.format", input_source.get("format", "unknown"))
            span.set_attribute("source.partition_count", input_source.get("partitions", 1))

            # 记录数据采集开始事件
            span.add_event("ingestion_start")

            # 模拟分块处理数据
            total_records = 0
            chunk_size = 10000
            chunk_count = 10

            for i in range(chunk_count):
                try:
                    # 处理每个数据块
                    processed = _process_source_chunk(i, chunk_size)
                    total_records += processed
                except Exception as e:
                    # 记录块处理失败，但继续处理其他块
                    span.add_event("chunk_failure_skipped", {
                        "chunk_id": i,
                        "error": str(e)
                    })
                    continue

            # 采集完成后记录结果摘要
            span.set_attribute("records.count", total_records)
            span.set_attribute("processing.success_rate",
                              round(total_records / (chunk_count * chunk_size) * 100, 2))

            span.add_event("ingestion_complete", {
                "records_processed": total_records,
                "status": "success",
                "processing_time_ms": int((time.time() - span.start_time) * 1000)
            })

            return {"status": "success", "count": total_records}
    except Exception as e:
        # 记录严重错误
        if 'span' in locals():
            span.set_status(Status(StatusCode.ERROR))
            span.record_exception(e)
            span.add_event("ingestion_failed", {
                "error_type": type(e).__name__,
                "error_message": str(e)
            })
        raise
    finally:
        if context and 'token' in locals():
            detach(token)

def data_transformation_stage(data, context=None):
    """数据转换阶段的高级追踪实现"""
    tracer = trace.get_tracer("data.transformation")

    try:
        if context:
            token = attach(DataPipelineTracer.extract_context(context))

        with tracer.start_as_current_span("data_transformation") as span:
            span.set_attribute("input.records", data["count"])

            # 记录转换开始事件
            span.add_event("transformation_start", {
                "transformations": ["normalize", "enrich", "validate"],
                "parallel_degree": 8
            })

            # 模拟不同的转换操作
            transformations = [
                ("normalize", 0.2),
                ("enrich", 0.5),
                ("validate", 0.3)
            ]

            for transform_name, weight in transformations:
                with tracer.start_as_current_span(f"transformation_{transform_name}") as child_span:
                    child_span.set_attribute("transformation.type", transform_name)

                    # 模拟转换延迟
                    processing_time = random.uniform(0.1 * weight, 0.5 * weight)
                    time.sleep(processing_time)

                    child_span.set_attribute("processing.time_ms", int(processing_time * 1000))

                    # 记录转换特定的属性
                    if transform_name == "validate":
                        validation_rate = random.uniform(0.95, 1.0)
                        child_span.set_attribute("validation.success_rate", round(validation_rate * 100, 2))

                    child_span.add_event(f"{transform_name}_complete")

            # 转换完成后记录结果
            transformed_records = int(data["count"] * random.uniform(0.95, 1.0))  # 模拟少量记录被过滤
            span.set_attribute("output.records", transformed_records)

            # 添加详细的转换指标
            total_time = (time.time() - span.start_time) * 1000
            span.set_attribute("transformation.time_ms", int(total_time))
            span.set_attribute("data.quality.valid", round(random.uniform(95, 99), 2))
            span.set_attribute("records.filtered", data["count"] - transformed_records)

            # 添加数据特征信息
            span.set_attribute("data.schema.version", "1.2.3")
            span.set_attribute("data.compression.ratio", round(random.uniform(0.3, 0.8), 2))

            span.add_event("transformation_complete", {
                "throughput_records_per_sec": int(transformed_records / (total_time / 1000))
            })

            return {"status": "success", "count": transformed_records}
    except Exception as e:
        # 记录错误信息
        if 'span' in locals():
            span.set_status(Status(StatusCode.ERROR))
            span.record_exception(e)
            span.add_event("transformation_failed", {
                "error": str(e),
                "transformation_stage": "unknown"
            })
        raise
    finally:
        if context and 'token' in locals():
            detach(token)

def data_loading_stage(data, target_config, context=None):
    """数据加载阶段的高级追踪实现"""
    tracer = trace.get_tracer("data.loading")

    try:
        if context:
            token = attach(DataPipelineTracer.extract_context(context))

        with tracer.start_as_current_span("data_loading") as span:
            span.set_attribute("input.records", data["count"])
            span.set_attribute("target.type", target_config["type"])
            span.set_attribute("target.location", target_config["location"])
            span.set_attribute("target.format", target_config.get("format", "parquet"))

            span.add_event("loading_start")

            # 模拟加载操作
            loading_time = random.uniform(0.5, 2.0)
            time.sleep(loading_time)

            # 模拟加载结果
            loaded_records = int(data["count"] * random.uniform(0.98, 1.0))

            # 记录加载指标
            span.set_attribute("output.records", loaded_records)
            span.set_attribute("loading.time_ms", int(loading_time * 1000))
            span.set_attribute("loading.success_rate", round(loaded_records / data["count"] * 100, 2))

            # 记录资源使用情况
            span.set_attribute("resource.memory_mb", random.randint(200, 500))
            span.set_attribute("resource.cpu_percent", round(random.uniform(40, 90), 2))

            span.add_event("loading_complete")

            return {"status": "success", "count": loaded_records}
    except Exception as e:
        if 'span' in locals():
            span.set_status(Status(StatusCode.ERROR))
            span.record_exception(e)
            span.add_event("loading_failed", {"error": str(e)})
        raise
    finally:
        if context and 'token' in locals():
            detach(token)

def execute_pipeline(input_source, target_config):
    """执行完整的数据处理流水线，包含高级追踪上下文传递"""
    tracer = trace.get_tracer("pipeline.coordinator")

    # 创建流水线执行ID
    pipeline_execution_id = f"pipeline-{int(time.time())}-{random.randint(1000, 9999)}"

    with tracer.start_as_current_span("pipeline_execution") as root_span:
        # 添加详细的元数据
        root_span.set_attribute("pipeline.name", "daily_data_processing")
        root_span.set_attribute("pipeline.execution_id", pipeline_execution_id)
        root_span.set_attribute("execution.timestamp", str(int(time.time())))
        root_span.set_attribute("execution.priority", "high")

        # 记录流水线配置
        root_span.add_event("pipeline_configured", {
            "input_source_type": input_source["type"],
            "target_type": target_config["type"],
            "pipeline_version": "1.2.0"
        })

        # 创建上下文用于传递追踪信息
        context = DataPipelineTracer.create_context()

        # 记录流水线开始
        root_span.add_event("pipeline_started")

        try:
            # 执行数据采集
            ingestion_start = time.time()
            root_span.add_event("ingestion_stage_started")
            ingestion_result = data_ingestion_stage(input_source, context)
            ingestion_duration = int((time.time() - ingestion_start) * 1000)

            root_span.add_event("ingestion_stage_completed", {
                "records_ingested": ingestion_result["count"],
                "duration_ms": ingestion_duration
            })

            # 执行数据转换
            transform_start = time.time()
            root_span.add_event("transformation_stage_started")
            transform_result = data_transformation_stage(ingestion_result, context)
            transform_duration = int((time.time() - transform_start) * 1000)

            root_span.add_event("transformation_stage_completed", {
                "records_transformed": transform_result["count"],
                "duration_ms": transform_duration
            })

            # 执行数据加载
            load_start = time.time()
            root_span.add_event("loading_stage_started")
            load_result = data_loading_stage(transform_result, target_config, context)
            load_duration = int((time.time() - load_start) * 1000)

            root_span.add_event("loading_stage_completed", {
                "records_loaded": load_result["count"],
                "duration_ms": load_duration
            })

            # 记录流水线性能指标
            total_duration = ingestion_duration + transform_duration + load_duration
            root_span.set_attribute("execution.duration_ms", total_duration)
            root_span.set_attribute("execution.success_rate",
                                  round(load_result["count"] / ingestion_result["count"] * 100, 2))
            root_span.set_attribute("throughput.records_per_sec",
                                  int(load_result["count"] / (total_duration / 1000)))

            # 完成整个流水线
            root_span.add_event("pipeline_completed", {
                "total_records_processed": load_result["count"],
                "status": "success",
                "stages_completed": 3,
                "total_duration_ms": total_duration
            })

            return {
                "status": "success",
                "count": load_result["count"],
                "execution_id": pipeline_execution_id,
                "duration_ms": total_duration
            }

        except Exception as e:
            # 记录流水线失败
            root_span.set_status(Status(StatusCode.ERROR))
            root_span.record_exception(e)
            root_span.add_event("pipeline_failed", {
                "error": str(e),
                "error_type": type(e).__name__
            })
            raise

# 示例用法
if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        # 定义输入源和目标配置
        input_config = {
            "type": "kafka",
            "location": "kafka-broker:9092",
            "format": "json",
            "partitions": 16
        }

        target_config = {
            "type": "hive",
            "location": "hdfs://namenode:8020/data/customer",
            "format": "parquet",
            "partition_by": ["date", "region"]
        }

        # 执行流水线
        result = execute_pipeline(input_config, target_config)
        print(f"流水线执行成功: {result}")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"流水线执行失败: {e}")
    finally:
        # 确保所有数据都被导出
        provider.force_flush()
```

**高级追踪配置与采样策略**：

```yaml
# OpenTelemetry Collector高级配置示例 - collector-config.yaml
receivers:
  # OTLP接收器配置
  otlp:
    protocols:
      grpc:
        endpoint: 0.0.0.0:4317
        # 大数据环境优化参数
        max_recv_msg_size_mib: 16
        read_buffer_size: 65536
      http:
        endpoint: 0.0.0.0:4318
        # 配置批处理和压缩
        cors:
          allowed_origins:

            - "*"

  # 直接从文件系统读取日志（可选）
  filelog:
    include:

      - /var/log/hadoop/*.log

      - /var/log/spark/*.log

    start_at: beginning

# 处理器配置
exporters:
  # Jaeger导出器
  jaeger:
    endpoint: jaeger-collector:14250
    tls:
      insecure: true
    # 配置重试策略
    retry_on_failure:
      enabled: true
      initial_interval: 5s
      max_interval: 30s
      max_elapsed_time: 10m

  # Elasticsearch导出器（用于长期存储和高级分析）
  elasticsearch:
    endpoints:

      - https://elasticsearch:9200

    index: "traces-%{+2006.01.02}"
    # 认证配置
    auth:
      username: ${ES_USERNAME}
      password: ${ES_PASSWORD}
    # 批处理配置
    bulk_size: 5000
    flush_interval: 10s

  # Zipkin导出器（兼容模式）
  zipkin:
    endpoint: "http://zipkin:9411/api/v2/spans"

  # 日志导出器
  logging:
    loglevel: info
    sampling_initial: 10
    sampling_thereafter: 100

processors:
  # 批处理处理器（减少网络调用和开销）
  batch:
    timeout: 1s
    send_batch_size: 1024
    send_batch_max_size: 2048

  # 内存限制处理器（防止OOM）
  memory_limiter:
    check_interval: 1s
    limit_mib: 1000
    spike_limit_mib: 200

  # 属性处理器（添加/修改/删除属性）
  attributes:
    actions:

      - key: environment

        action: upsert
        value: production

      - key: cluster

        action: upsert
        value: data-lake-cluster-1

      - key: sensitive_data

        action: delete

  # 资源处理器（修改资源属性）
  resource:
    attributes:

      - key: deployment.environment

        action: upsert
        value: production

  # 先进的采样配置
  probabilistic_sampler:
    hash_seed: 22
    sampling_percentage: 5.0  # 默认5%采样率，大数据环境降低采样率

  # 尾部采样（智能采样策略）
  tail_sampling:
    decision_wait: 10s
    num_traces: 10000
    expected_new_traces_per_sec: 2000
    policies:
      # 1. 错误优先采样 - 100%采样包含错误的追踪

      - name: error_policy

        type: status_code
        status_code:
          status_codes: [ERROR]

      # 2. 高延迟采样 - 采样处理时间超过2秒的追踪

      - name: latency_policy

        type: latency
        latency:
          threshold_ms: 2000
          decision_wait: 5s

      # 3. 关键服务采样 - 对关键服务提高采样率

      - name: critical_service_policy

        type: string_attribute
        string_attribute:
          key: service.name
          values:

            - "data-ingestion-service"

            - "data-quality-service"

          enabled_regex_matching: true

      # 4. 特定操作采样 - 对关键操作提高采样率

      - name: critical_operation_policy

        type: string_attribute
        string_attribute:
          key: operation.name
          values:

            - "pipeline_execution"

            - "data_loading"

          enabled_regex_matching: true

      # 5. 速率限制采样 - 确保系统负载可控

      - name: rate_limit_policy

        type: rate_limiting
        rate_limiting:
          spans_per_second: 50

  # 追踪缩减处理器（减少数据量）
  spanprocessor:
    include_all_attributes: false
    include_all_events: false
    include_all_links: false
    attributes:
      actions:

        - key: user_id

          action: hash

        - key: ip_address

          action: hash

# 针对不同信号的服务管道配置
service:
  pipelines:
    # 追踪管道
    traces:
      receivers: [otlp]
      processors:

        - memory_limiter

        - attributes

        - resource

        - probabilistic_sampler

        - tail_sampling

        - spanprocessor

        - batch

      exporters: [jaeger, elasticsearch]

    # 指标管道
    metrics:
      receivers: [otlp]
      processors: [memory_limiter, attributes, resource, batch]
      exporters: [prometheus, logging]

    # 日志管道
    logs:
      receivers: [otlp, filelog]
      processors: [memory_limiter, attributes, resource, batch]
      exporters: [elasticsearch, logging]
```

**大数据环境分布式追踪最佳实践**：

1. **智能采样策略**：

   - 对错误和异常100%采样，确保问题可追踪

   - 对关键业务流程和服务提高采样率

   - 对常规流量使用低采样率（1-5%）控制数据量

   - 基于延迟的动态采样，捕获性能异常的追踪

2. **上下文传播强化**：

   - 在所有服务间一致地传递追踪上下文

   - 支持多种传播协议（W3C TraceContext、B3等）

   - 确保在异步处理、消息队列和批处理任务中正确传播上下文

3. **资源和性能优化**：

   - 使用批处理减少网络开销

   - 实施适当的内存限制防止OOM

   - 过滤敏感信息保护数据隐私

   - 对高频属性使用哈希处理减少存储压力

4. **数据保留和存储策略**：

   - 热数据短期保留在高性能存储中

   - 冷数据长期归档到低成本存储

   - 对关键追踪和错误追踪延长保留期

   - 使用索引生命周期管理自动归档和清理

5. **集成与关联**：

   - 将追踪数据与指标和日志关联，实现三大支柱的统一视图

   - 使用相同的标签和标识符跨不同信号关联数据

   - 构建端到端的数据血缘和追踪视图

   - 实现自动异常检测和根因分析

6. **可扩展架构**：

   - 采用水平扩展的收集器架构

   - 使用负载均衡分散收集器压力

   - 实施熔断机制确保追踪系统故障不影响主业务

   - 定期进行容量规划和性能测试

#### 指标监控 （来自：第4篇-第13章-大数据系统可观测性与监控测试【进阶】）

- **概念**：指标监控通过收集系统各层面的数值型数据，反映系统运行状态和趋势，支持告警和性能分析。在大数据环境中，指标监控尤为关键，因为大数据系统通常具有规模庞大、组件复杂、数据流量高、业务依赖强等特点。有效的指标监控需要覆盖从基础设施到应用层的全方位指标，并能够处理高基数、高维度和高 cardinality的数据特性，同时保持系统自身的轻量级和高性能。

- **指标类型**：

  - **计数器（Counter）**：累积型指标，持续增加且不会减少，如请求总数、错误数、处理记录数等

  - **仪表盘（Gauge）**：瞬时值指标，可以上升或下降，如CPU使用率、内存使用量、队列长度等

  - **直方图（Histogram）**：分布型指标，用于观察数值分布情况，如请求延迟分布、处理时间分布等

  - **摘要（Summary）**：统计型指标，直接计算并存储分位数，如延迟的P50/P95/P99分位数等

- **主流工具**：Prometheus、Grafana、Datadog、InfluxDB、New Relic、Elastic APM

- **应用场景**：系统健康监控、性能趋势分析、资源利用评估、自动告警、容量规划、成本优化、数据质量监控

**大数据环境中的高级指标监控实现示例**：

```python
# 大数据处理系统的高级指标监控实现
from prometheus_client import Counter, Gauge, Histogram, Summary, start_http_server, Info, Enum
import time
import random
import threading
from contextlib import contextmanager
from functools import wraps
import psutil  # 用于获取真实系统资源指标

# ================ 1. 基础指标定义 ================

# 数据处理计数器 - 带标签增强
PROCESSING_COUNTER = Counter(
    'bigdata_processing_total',
    'Total number of data records processed',
    ['pipeline', 'stage', 'status', 'data_source', 'job_type']
)

# 错误计数器 - 按错误类型分类
ERROR_COUNTER = Counter(
    'bigdata_errors_total',
    'Total number of errors by type',
    ['pipeline', 'stage', 'error_type', 'severity']
)

# 数据处理延迟直方图 - 优化的桶配置
PROCESSING_HISTOGRAM = Histogram(
    'bigdata_processing_seconds',
    'Time spent processing data',
    ['pipeline', 'stage', 'data_volume'],
    buckets=(0.01, 0.05, 0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 5.0, 10.0, 30.0, 60.0, 120.0, 300.0, 600.0)
)

# 数据质量摘要 - 多维度评估
DATA_QUALITY_SUMMARY = Summary(
    'bigdata_data_quality_score',
    'Data quality score for processed data',
    ['pipeline', 'data_type', 'quality_dimension']  # 质量维度：完整性、准确性、一致性等
)

# 系统资源仪表盘 - 增强版
RESOURCE_GAUGE = Gauge(
    'bigdata_resource_usage',
    'Resource usage for big data processing',
    ['resource_type', 'component', 'host', 'instance']
)

# 队列长度仪表盘
QUEUE_LENGTH_GAUGE = Gauge(
    'bigdata_queue_length',
    'Length of processing queues',
    ['queue_name', 'priority']
)

# 并行度仪表盘
PARALLELISM_GAUGE = Gauge(
    'bigdata_task_parallelism',
    'Current parallelism level of tasks',
    ['pipeline', 'resource_pool']
)

# 版本信息指标
VERSION_INFO = Info(
    'bigdata_pipeline_version',
    'Version information about the data pipeline'
)

# 任务状态枚举
TASK_STATUS_ENUM = Enum(
    'bigdata_task_status',
    'Current status of data processing tasks',
    states=['idle', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed', 'retrying'],
    labelnames=['pipeline', 'task_id']
)

# 数据量大小直方图 - 跟踪不同大小的数据批次
DATA_VOLUME_HISTOGRAM = Histogram(
    'bigdata_batch_size_bytes',
    'Size of data batches processed',
    ['pipeline', 'stage'],
    buckets=(1024, 10240, 102400, 1048576, 10485760, 104857600, 1048576000)
)

# 缓存命中率计数器
CACHE_HIT_COUNTER = Counter(
    'bigdata_cache_operations_total',
    'Cache hit/miss operations',
    ['cache_name', 'operation_type']  # operation_type: hit, miss, evict
)

# ================ 2. 工具函数与装饰器 ================

@contextmanager
def measure_processing_time(pipeline, stage, data_volume='medium'):
    """用于测量和记录处理时间的上下文管理器"""
    start_time = time.time()
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        PROCESSING_HISTOGRAM.labels(
            pipeline=pipeline,
            stage=stage,
            data_volume=data_volume
        ).observe(time.time() - start_time)

def monitor_method(pipeline, stage):
    """用于监控方法执行的装饰器"""
    def decorator(func):
        @wraps(func)
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            # 记录方法开始执行
            TASK_STATUS_ENUM.labels(pipeline=pipeline, task_id=f"{stage}-{id(func)}").state('running')

            try:
                # 测量执行时间
                with measure_processing_time(pipeline, stage):
                    result = func(*args, **kwargs)

                # 记录成功状态
                TASK_STATUS_ENUM.labels(pipeline=pipeline, task_id=f"{stage}-{id(func)}").state('succeeded')
                return result
            except Exception as e:
                # 记录错误
                error_type = type(e).__name__
                ERROR_COUNTER.labels(
                    pipeline=pipeline,
                    stage=stage,
                    error_type=error_type,
                    severity='critical' if 'fatal' in str(e).lower() else 'warning'
                ).inc()

                # 更新任务状态为失败
                TASK_STATUS_ENUM.labels(pipeline=pipeline, task_id=f"{stage}-{id(func)}").state('failed')
                raise
        return wrapper
    return decorator

# ================ 3. 数据处理模拟与监控 ================

class DataPipelineMonitor:
    """数据流水线监控类"""

    def __init__(self, pipeline_name):
        self.pipeline_name = pipeline_name
        # 设置版本信息
        VERSION_INFO.info({
            'pipeline': pipeline_name,
            'version': '1.2.3',
            'build_date': '2023-10-23',
            'environment': 'production'
        })

    @monitor_method(pipeline='etl_pipeline', stage='extract')
    def extract_data(self, source_type, record_count):
        """提取数据阶段"""
        # 模拟提取延迟
        time.sleep(random.uniform(0.2, 1.0))

        # 模拟不同的数据量
        batch_size_bytes = random.randint(10240, 104857600)
        DATA_VOLUME_HISTOGRAM.labels(
            pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
            stage='extract'
        ).observe(batch_size_bytes)

        # 模拟成功率
        success_rate = random.uniform(0.9, 0.99)
        successful_records = int(record_count * success_rate)
        failed_records = record_count - successful_records

        # 更新计数器
        PROCESSING_COUNTER.labels(
            pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
            stage='extract',
            status='success',
            data_source=source_type,
            job_type='batch'
        ).inc(successful_records)

        if failed_records > 0:
            PROCESSING_COUNTER.labels(
                pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
                stage='extract',
                status='failure',
                data_source=source_type,
                job_type='batch'
            ).inc(failed_records)

        # 更新数据质量指标 - 完整性
        DATA_QUALITY_SUMMARY.labels(
            pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
            data_type=source_type,
            quality_dimension='completeness'
        ).observe(success_rate)

        return {"status": "success", "processed": successful_records, "failed": failed_records}

    @monitor_method(pipeline='etl_pipeline', stage='transform')
    def transform_data(self, input_data, transformations=None):
        """转换数据阶段"""
        # 模拟转换延迟
        time.sleep(random.uniform(0.5, 2.0))

        # 模拟不同转换操作的成功率
        transformations = transformations or ['clean', 'normalize', 'enrich', 'validate']
        for transform in transformations:
            # 为每个转换操作记录质量指标
            quality_score = random.uniform(0.85, 1.0)
            DATA_QUALITY_SUMMARY.labels(
                pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
                data_type='transformed',
                quality_dimension=transform
            ).observe(quality_score)

        # 模拟整体转换成功率
        success_rate = random.uniform(0.92, 0.98)
        successful_records = int(input_data['processed'] * success_rate)
        failed_records = input_data['processed'] - successful_records

        # 更新计数器
        PROCESSING_COUNTER.labels(
            pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
            stage='transform',
            status='success',
            data_source='internal',
            job_type='batch'
        ).inc(successful_records)

        if failed_records > 0:
            PROCESSING_COUNTER.labels(
                pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
                stage='transform',
                status='failure',
                data_source='internal',
                job_type='batch'
            ).inc(failed_records)

        return {"status": "success", "processed": successful_records, "failed": failed_records}

    @monitor_method(pipeline='etl_pipeline', stage='load')
    def load_data(self, input_data, target_type):
        """加载数据阶段"""
        # 模拟加载延迟
        time.sleep(random.uniform(0.3, 1.5))

        # 模拟加载成功率
        success_rate = random.uniform(0.95, 1.0)
        successful_records = int(input_data['processed'] * success_rate)
        failed_records = input_data['processed'] - successful_records

        # 更新计数器
        PROCESSING_COUNTER.labels(
            pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
            stage='load',
            status='success',
            data_source='internal',
            job_type='batch'
        ).inc(successful_records)

        if failed_records > 0:
            PROCESSING_COUNTER.labels(
                pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
                stage='load',
                status='failure',
                data_source='internal',
                job_type='batch'
            ).inc(failed_records)

        # 更新数据质量指标 - 一致性
        DATA_QUALITY_SUMMARY.labels(
            pipeline=self.pipeline_name,
            data_type=target_type,
            quality_dimension='consistency'
        ).observe(success_rate)

        return {"status": "success", "processed": successful_records, "failed": failed_records}

def update_system_resources(interval=5):
    """更新系统资源指标的后台线程函数"""
    def _update_loop():
        while True:
            # 获取真实系统资源指标
            try:
                # CPU使用率
                cpu_usage = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1)
                RESOURCE_GAUGE.labels(
                    resource_type='cpu',
                    component='spark_executor',
                    host='localhost',
                    instance='executor-1'
                ).set(cpu_usage)

                # 内存使用率
                memory = psutil.virtual_memory()
                RESOURCE_GAUGE.labels(
                    resource_type='memory',
                    component='spark_executor',
                    host='localhost',
                    instance='executor-1'
                ).set(memory.percent)

                # 磁盘I/O
                disk_io = psutil.disk_io_counters()
                # 转换为每秒读写字节数
                # 注意：这里需要计算速率，简化处理
                RESOURCE_GAUGE.labels(
                    resource_type='disk_read_bytes',
                    component='hdfs_datanode',
                    host='localhost',
                    instance='datanode-1'
                ).set(disk_io.read_bytes / (interval * 1024 * 1024))  # MB

                RESOURCE_GAUGE.labels(
                    resource_type='disk_write_bytes',
                    component='hdfs_datanode',
                    host='localhost',
                    instance='datanode-1'
                ).set(disk_io.write_bytes / (interval * 1024 * 1024))  # MB

                # 网络流量
                net_io = psutil.net_io_counters()
                RESOURCE_GAUGE.labels(
                    resource_type='network_recv_bytes',
                    component='cluster',
                    host='localhost',
                    instance='network-1'
                ).set(net_io.bytes_recv / (interval * 1024 * 1024))  # MB

                RESOURCE_GAUGE.labels(
                    resource_type='network_sent_bytes',
                    component='cluster',
                    host='localhost',
                    instance='network-1'
                ).set(net_io.bytes_sent / (interval * 1024 * 1024))  # MB

                # 模拟队列长度
                QUEUE_LENGTH_GAUGE.labels(
                    queue_name='ingestion_queue',
                    priority='high'
                ).set(random.randint(0, 1000))

                # 模拟并行度
                PARALLELISM_GAUGE.labels(
                    pipeline='etl_pipeline',
                    resource_pool='default'
                ).set(random.randint(1, 32))

                # 模拟缓存操作
                if random.random() < 0.7:  # 70%概率缓存命中
                    CACHE_HIT_COUNTER.labels(
                        cache_name='data_lookup_cache',
                        operation_type='hit'
                    ).inc()
                else:
                    CACHE_HIT_COUNTER.labels(
                        cache_name='data_lookup_cache',
                        operation_type='miss'
                    ).inc()

                # 偶尔模拟缓存驱逐
                if random.random() < 0.05:
                    CACHE_HIT_COUNTER.labels(
                        cache_name='data_lookup_cache',
                        operation_type='evict'
                    ).inc()

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"更新资源指标失败: {e}")
                # 记录错误但继续运行
                ERROR_COUNTER.labels(
                    pipeline='monitoring',
                    stage='resource_collection',
                    error_type=type(e).__name__,
                    severity='warning'
                ).inc()

            time.sleep(interval)

    # 启动后台线程
    thread = threading.Thread(target=_update_loop, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    return thread

# ================ 4. 主执行逻辑 ================

def main():
    # 启动指标HTTP服务器
    start_http_server(8000)
    print("指标服务器启动在 http://localhost:8000/metrics")

    # 启动资源监控线程
    resource_thread = update_system_resources(interval=5)

    # 创建监控实例
    monitor = DataPipelineMonitor('etl_pipeline')

    # 模拟持续的数据处理
    pipeline_runs = 0
    try:
        while True:
            pipeline_runs += 1
            print(f"\n执行流水线运行 #{pipeline_runs}")

            # 模拟数据提取
            extract_result = monitor.extract_data(
                source_type=random.choice(['kafka', 'hdfs', 'database', 'api']),
                record_count=random.randint(5000, 20000)
            )
            print(f"提取结果: 成功={extract_result['processed']}, 失败={extract_result['failed']}")

            # 模拟数据转换
            transform_result = monitor.transform_data(
                input_data=extract_result,
                transformations=random.sample(['clean', 'normalize', 'enrich', 'validate', 'aggregate'],
                                            k=random.randint(2, 5))
            )
            print(f"转换结果: 成功={transform_result['processed']}, 失败={transform_result['failed']}")

            # 模拟数据加载
            load_result = monitor.load_data(
                input_data=transform_result,
                target_type=random.choice(['hive', 'elasticsearch', 'data_lake', 'data_mart'])
            )
            print(f"加载结果: 成功={load_result['processed']}, 失败={load_result['failed']}")

            # 随机模拟异常情况
            if random.random() < 0.1:  # 10%概率触发异常
                print("模拟异常情况...")
                # 故意调用一个会失败的方法
                with measure_processing_time(pipeline='etl_pipeline', stage='error_test'):
                    raise Exception("模拟的数据处理错误")

            # 等待一段时间再执行下一次
            sleep_time = random.uniform(2, 5)
            print(f"等待 {sleep_time:.2f} 秒...")
            time.sleep(sleep_time)

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n接收到中断信号，正在退出...")
    finally:
        print("程序已退出")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 在实际环境中，psutil需要安装: pip install psutil
    main()
```

**高级Prometheus告警规则配置**：

```yaml
# 大数据系统高级告警规则 - advanced_alert_rules.yml
groups:

- name: bigdata_alerts

  rules:
  # 1. 数据处理告警

  - alert: HighProcessingErrorRate

    expr: sum(rate(bigdata_processing_total{status="failure"}[5m])) / sum(rate(bigdata_processing_total[5m])) > 0.05
    for: 2m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: data_quality
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "高数据处理错误率 ({{ $value | humanizePercentage }})"
      description: "数据处理错误率超过5%已持续2分钟\n流水线: {{ $labels.pipeline }}\n请检查相关日志和处理逻辑"
      runbook_url: "https://wiki.example.com/runbooks/high-error-rate"

  - alert: CriticalProcessingErrorRate

    expr: sum(rate(bigdata_processing_total{status="failure"}[5m])) / sum(rate(bigdata_processing_total[5m])) > 0.15
    for: 1m
    labels:
      severity: critical
      category: data_quality
      team: data_engineering
      pagerduty: "data-pipeline-alerts"
    annotations:
      summary: "严重数据处理错误率 ({{ $value | humanizePercentage }})"
      description: "数据处理错误率超过15%已持续1分钟\n流水线: {{ $labels.pipeline }}\n这是紧急情况，需要立即处理！"
      runbook_url: "https://wiki.example.com/runbooks/critical-error-rate"
      dashboard_url: "{{ $labels.pipeline | dashboardLink }}"

  # 2. 资源使用告警

  - alert: HighCPUUsage

    expr: bigdata_resource_usage{resource_type="cpu"} > 85
    for: 5m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: resource
      team: infrastructure
    annotations:
      summary: "高CPU使用率 ({{ $value }}%)"
      description: "组件 {{ $labels.component }} 的CPU使用率超过85%已持续5分钟\n主机: {{ $labels.host }}\n实例: {{ $labels.instance }}"

  - alert: HighMemoryUsage

    expr: bigdata_resource_usage{resource_type="memory"} > 90
    for: 3m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: resource
      team: infrastructure
    annotations:
      summary: "高内存使用率 ({{ $value }}%)"
      description: "组件 {{ $labels.component }} 的内存使用率超过90%已持续3分钟\n主机: {{ $labels.host }}\n实例: {{ $labels.instance }}\n可能需要增加资源或检查内存泄漏"

  - alert: CriticalDiskIOWait

    expr: rate(node_disk_io_time_seconds_total[5m]) > 0.8
    for: 2m
    labels:
      severity: critical
      category: resource
      team: infrastructure
    annotations:
      summary: "严重磁盘I/O等待时间"
      description: "磁盘I/O等待时间超过80%已持续2分钟\n这可能导致数据处理严重延迟"

  # 3. 性能告警

  - alert: HighProcessingLatency

    expr: histogram_quantile(0.95, sum(rate(bigdata_processing_seconds_bucket[5m])) by (le, pipeline, stage)) > 30
    for: 3m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: performance
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "高处理延迟 ({{ $value }}s)"
      description: "流水线 {{ $labels.pipeline }} 的 {{ $labels.stage }} 阶段P95延迟超过30秒\n请检查数据量和资源分配"

  - alert: ProcessingLatencyDegradation

    expr: |
      histogram_quantile(0.95, sum(rate(bigdata_processing_seconds_bucket[5m])) by (le, pipeline, stage)) /
      histogram_quantile(0.95, sum(rate(bigdata_processing_seconds_bucket[1h] offset 24h)) by (le, pipeline, stage)) > 1.5
    for: 5m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: performance
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "处理延迟性能下降"
      description: "流水线 {{ $labels.pipeline }} 的 {{ $labels.stage }} 阶段延迟比24小时前增加了50%以上\n当前P95: {{ $value | humanizeDuration }}"

  # 4. 数据质量告警

  - alert: LowDataQualityScore

    expr: bigdata_data_quality_score_sum / bigdata_data_quality_score_count < 0.85
    for: 5m
    labels:
      severity: critical
      category: data_quality
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "低数据质量分数 ({{ $value | humanizePercentage }})"
      description: "流水线 {{ $labels.pipeline }} 的 {{ $labels.data_type }} 数据质量分数低于85%\n质量维度: {{ $labels.quality_dimension }}\n请检查数据源和处理逻辑"

  - alert: DataQualityAnomaly

    expr: |
      abs(
        (bigdata_data_quality_score_sum / bigdata_data_quality_score_count) -
        avg_over_time((bigdata_data_quality_score_sum / bigdata_data_quality_score_count)[24h:5m])
      ) > 0.15
    for: 10m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: data_quality
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "数据质量异常波动"
      description: "流水线 {{ $labels.pipeline }} 的数据质量分数与24小时平均值相比波动超过15%\n当前值: {{ $value | humanizePercentage }}\n可能表示数据源发生变化"

  # 5. 队列积压告警

  - alert: QueueBacklogHigh

    expr: bigdata_queue_length > 5000
    for: 5m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: performance
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "队列积压严重"
      description: "队列 {{ $labels.queue_name }} (优先级: {{ $labels.priority }}) 的长度超过5000已持续5分钟\n可能导致处理延迟增加"

  - alert: QueueBacklogCritical

    expr: bigdata_queue_length > 10000
    for: 3m
    labels:
      severity: critical
      category: performance
      team: data_engineering
      pagerduty: "data-pipeline-alerts"
    annotations:
      summary: "队列积压严重(危急)"
      description: "队列 {{ $labels.queue_name }} (优先级: {{ $labels.priority }}) 的长度超过10000已持续3分钟\n需要立即增加处理能力或检查下游系统"

  # 6. 缓存效率告警

  - alert: LowCacheHitRatio

    expr: |
      sum(rate(bigdata_cache_operations_total{operation_type="hit"}[5m])) /
      (sum(rate(bigdata_cache_operations_total{operation_type="hit"}[5m])) +
       sum(rate(bigdata_cache_operations_total{operation_type="miss"}[5m])))
      < 0.6
    for: 10m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: performance
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "缓存命中率低"
      description: "缓存 {{ $labels.cache_name }} 的命中率低于60%\n考虑优化缓存策略或增加缓存容量"

  # 7. 任务状态告警

  - alert: TaskFailureRate

    expr: sum(rate(bigdata_errors_total[5m])) by (pipeline) > 10
    for: 2m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: reliability
      team: data_engineering
    annotations:
      summary: "任务失败率高"
      description: "流水线 {{ $labels.pipeline }} 的错误率超过每分钟10次\n请检查系统日志和监控面板"

  # 8. 资源利用率异常告警

  - alert: ResourceUtilizationAnomaly

    expr: |
      abs(
        bigdata_resource_usage{resource_type="cpu"} -
        avg_over_time(bigdata_resource_usage{resource_type="cpu"}[24h:5m])
      ) > 30
    for: 15m
    labels:
      severity: warning
      category: resource
      team: infrastructure
    annotations:
      summary: "CPU使用率异常波动"
      description: "组件 {{ $labels.component }} 的CPU使用率与24小时平均值相比变化超过30%\n当前值: {{ $value }}%\n可能表示异常活动或配置问题"
```

**大数据环境下的指标监控最佳实践**：

1. **指标设计原则**：

   - **多维度标签设计**：为指标添加丰富的标签，便于多维度分析和过滤

   - **指标粒度平衡**：在详细度和性能之间找到平衡，避免过高的基数

   - **命名规范统一**：采用一致的命名约定，如`系统_模块_度量_单位`格式

   - **关键指标优先**：优先监控业务核心指标和系统关键性能指标

2. **大规模部署策略**：

   - **分层指标收集**：采用层次化架构，从基础设施到应用层逐层收集

   - **联邦集群**：使用Prometheus联邦集群架构，水平扩展监控能力

   - **高可用设计**：确保监控系统自身的高可用性，避免单点故障

   - **抓取优化**：调整抓取间隔和超时设置，平衡监控精度和系统负载

3. **性能与资源优化**：

   - **批量处理**：使用批处理减少监控数据的传输和存储开销

   - **采样策略**：对高频指标实施智能采样，降低采集和存储压力

   - **数据压缩**：启用数据压缩，减少网络和存储消耗

   - **TTL设置**：为不同重要性的指标设置合理的保留期限

4. **告警策略优化**：

   - **多级告警**：根据严重程度设置不同级别的告警（信息、警告、严重、紧急）

   - **告警聚合**：对相似告警进行聚合，避免告警风暴

   - **告警抑制**：配置合理的告警抑制规则，避免级联告警

   - **告警自愈**：对常见问题实现自动修复流程，减少人工干预

5. **可视化最佳实践**：

   - **多层仪表盘**：构建从概览到详情的多层仪表盘结构

   - **业务关联**：将技术指标与业务指标关联展示，体现业务影响

   - **异常标注**：在图表中自动标注异常事件和告警

   - **历史对比**：提供与历史数据的对比视图，便于识别异常模式

6. **数据关联与整合**：

   - **三大支柱整合**：将指标与日志、追踪数据关联分析

   - **统一ID体系**：在不同数据源之间使用统一的标识符（如追踪ID）

   - **上下文丰富**：将指标数据与业务上下文、配置信息关联

   - **元数据管理**：建立完善的指标元数据管理机制

7. **安全与合规**：

   - **访问控制**：实施细粒度的访问控制和权限管理

   - **数据脱敏**：对敏感指标数据进行脱敏处理

   - **审计日志**：记录监控系统自身的访问和操作日志

   - **合规监控**：建立专门的合规监控指标和仪表盘

8. **持续优化**：

   - **指标评估**：定期评估指标的有效性和使用价值

   - **成本优化**：优化存储和查询成本，实施冷热数据分离

   - **告警优化**：基于误报率和漏报率持续优化告警规则

   - **自动化扩展**：根据业务增长自动扩展监控能力

```

#### 日志分析 （来自：第4篇-第13章-大数据系统可观测性与监控测试【进阶】）

- **概念**：日志分析通过收集和分析系统产生的日志数据，提供系统行为的详细记录和上下文信息。在大数据环境中，日志分析面临着独特挑战：

  - **数据规模爆炸**：每天产生TB/PB级日志数据

  - **组件异构性**：Hadoop、Spark、Kafka等多种组件的日志格式和标准各异

  - **实时性要求**：流式处理场景需要毫秒级的日志处理能力

  - **高可用压力**：日志系统本身需要极高的可用性保障

  - **存储成本**：原始日志存储成本高昂，需要高效的压缩和归档策略

- **日志类型**：

  - **应用日志**：业务逻辑执行过程中的记录，包含业务事件、用户行为、异常堆栈等

  - **系统日志**：操作系统、容器、集群管理器的运行状态和资源使用情况

  - **安全日志**：认证授权事件、访问控制、异常行为检测相关记录

  - **审计日志**：满足合规性要求的关键操作记录，支持追溯和责任认定

  - **性能日志**：组件级和端到端的性能指标、瓶颈标识数据

- **日志格式**：

  - **结构化日志**：JSON、XML等，便于机器解析和索引

  - **半结构化日志**：包含固定字段和自由文本的混合格式

  - **非结构化日志**：纯文本格式，需要正则表达式或NLP技术解析

- **主流工具**：

  - **ELK Stack**（Elasticsearch, Logstash, Kibana）：最广泛使用的开源日志分析平台

  - **Loki**：Grafana生态的轻量级日志聚合系统

  - **Splunk**：企业级商业日志分析平台

  - **Graylog**：专注于搜索性能的日志管理系统

  - **Fluentd/Fluent Bit**：高性能日志收集和转发工具

  - **Vector**：Rust编写的高性能可观测性数据收集器

- **应用场景**：

  - **问题根因分析**：快速定位分布式系统故障源头

  - **性能优化**：识别系统瓶颈和性能异常

  - **安全审计**：检测潜在安全威胁和异常访问

  - **合规性检查**：满足行业监管和数据治理要求

  - **行为分析**：用户行为模式和系统使用趋势分析

**大数据环境中的日志收集与处理示例**：

```python

# 使用Python实现大数据环境下的结构化日志收集和处理
import logging
import json
import time
import random
import threading
import queue
import uuid
import traceback
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime
from elasticsearch import Elasticsearch, helpers
from confluent_kafka import Producer, KafkaError
from opentelemetry import trace
from opentelemetry.sdk.resources import SERVICE_NAME, Resource
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace import TracerProvider
from opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import BatchSpanProcessor
from opentelemetry.exporter.otlp.proto.grpc.trace_exporter import
OTLPSpanExporter

# 初始化OpenTelemetry追踪器，实现日志与追踪的关联
resource = Resource(attributes={SERVICE_NAME: "bigdata-logging-service"})
trace_provider = TracerProvider(resource=resource)
trace_provider.add_span_processor(BatchSpanProcessor(OTLPSpanExporter(endpoint="http://otel-collector:4317")))
trace.set_tracer_provider(trace_provider)
tracer = trace.get_tracer("bigdata.pipeline")

# 高级结构化日志格式化器
class EnhancedJSONFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    def __init__(self, service_name="bigdata-pipeline", version="1.0.0", environment="production"):
        super().__init__()
        self.service_name = service_name
        self.version = version
        self.environment = environment

    def format(self, record):
        # 基础日志结构
        log_data = {
            "@timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "level": record.levelname,
            "service": record.name,
            "message": record.getMessage(),
            "component": record.module,
            "line": record.lineno,
            "thread": {
                "id": record.thread,
                "name": record.threadName
            },
            "process": {
                "id": record.process,
                "name": record.processName
            },
            # 标准字段添加
            "service.name": self.service_name,
            "service.version": self.version,
            "environment": self.environment
        }

        # 从记录中提取追踪信息（支持与OpenTelemetry集成）
        if hasattr(record, 'trace_id'):
            log_data["trace.id"] = record.trace_id
            # 为高基数字段生成哈希，优化存储性能
            log_data["trace.id_hash"] = hashlib.md5(str(record.trace_id).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]

        if hasattr(record, 'span_id'):
            log_data["span.id"] = record.span_id

        # 增加异常信息（结构化格式）
        if record.exc_info:
            log_data["error"] = {
                "type": record.exc_info[0].__name__ if record.exc_info[0] else "Unknown",
                "message": str(record.exc_info[1]) if record.exc_info[1] else "Unknown",
                "stack_trace": self.formatException(record.exc_info)
            }

        # 增加大数据处理特定字段
        for field in ['pipeline', 'stage', 'record_count', 'processing_time_ms',
                      'data_quality', 'event_type', 'partition', 'retry_count']:
            if hasattr(record, field):
                log_data[field] = getattr(record, field)

        # 增加自定义字段（通过extra传入）
        if hasattr(record, 'custom_fields'):
            log_data.update(record.custom_fields)

        return json.dumps(log_data)

# 配置高级日志记录器
class LoggingConfig:
    @staticmethod
    def get_logger(name="bigdata-pipeline", level=logging.INFO):
        logger = logging.getLogger(name)
        logger.setLevel(level)

        # 避免重复添加处理器
        if not logger.handlers:
            # 控制台处理器
            console_handler = logging.StreamHandler()
            console_handler.setFormatter(EnhancedJSONFormatter())
            logger.addHandler(console_handler)

            # 文件处理器（轮转）
            file_handler = logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler(
                f"logs/{name}.log",
                maxBytes=100 * 1024 * 1024,  # 100MB
                backupCount=10
            )
            file_handler.setFormatter(EnhancedJSONFormatter())
            logger.addHandler(file_handler)

        return logger

logger = LoggingConfig.get_logger()

# 健壮的外部系统连接器
class ExternalConnectors:
    # 配置连接池和重试策略
    @staticmethod
    def get_elasticsearch_client():
        return Elasticsearch(
            hosts=["http://elasticsearch:9200"],
            max_retries=5,
            retry_on_timeout=True,
            timeout=30,
            # 连接池优化
            connections_per_node=5,
            sniff_on_start=True,
            sniff_on_connection_fail=True,
            sniffer_timeout=60
        )

    @staticmethod
    def get_kafka_producer():
        return Producer({
            'bootstrap.servers': 'kafka:9092',
            'client.id': 'bigdata-log-producer',
            # 性能优化配置
            'linger.ms': 100,
            'batch.size': 1024 * 1024,  # 1MB
            'compression.type': 'snappy',
            # 重试策略
            'retries': 5,
            'retry.backoff.ms': 100
        })

# 初始化外部连接器
try:
    es_client = ExternalConnectors.get_elasticsearch_client()
    kafka_producer = ExternalConnectors.get_kafka_producer()
except Exception as e:
    logger.error(f"初始化外部连接失败: {str(e)}")
    # 降级处理：使用空实现
    es_client = None
    kafka_producer = None

# 高级日志处理队列和线程池
class AdvancedLogProcessor:
    def __init__(self, queue_size=100000, worker_count=4):
        self.queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=queue_size)
        self.workers = []
        self.running = False
        self.worker_count = worker_count
        self.stats_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.stats = {
            "processed": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "batch_size": 0
        }

    def start(self):
        """启动日志处理工作线程"""
        self.running = True
        for i in range(self.worker_count):
            worker = threading.Thread(target=self._worker_thread, args=(i,))
            worker.daemon = True
            worker.start()
            self.workers.append(worker)
        logger.info(f"启动了 {self.worker_count} 个日志处理工作线程")

    def stop(self):
        """优雅停止所有工作线程"""
        self.running = False
        for worker in self.workers:
            worker.join(timeout=5)
        logger.info(f"日志处理线程已停止，处理统计: {self.stats}")

    def _worker_thread(self, worker_id):
        """工作线程实现，包含错误处理和重试逻辑"""
        batch_size = 500
        batch_timeout = 0.5
        batch = []
        last_batch_time = time.time()

        while self.running:
            try:
                # 尝试获取日志条目
                try:
                    log_entry = self.queue.get(timeout=1)
                    batch.append(log_entry)
                except queue.Empty:
                    pass

                # 检查批处理条件
                current_time = time.time()
                if (len(batch) >= batch_size or
                    (batch and current_time - last_batch_time >= batch_timeout)):
                    # 执行批量处理
                    self._process_batch(batch.copy(), worker_id)
                    batch = []
                    last_batch_time = current_time

            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"工作线程 {worker_id} 异常: {str(e)}",
                           extra={"exception": traceback.format_exc()})
                time.sleep(0.1)  # 避免忙等

    def _process_batch(self, batch, worker_id):
        """批量处理日志条目，支持多种输出目标"""
        if not batch:
            return

        try:
            # 1. 批量发送到Elasticsearch
            if es_client:
                try:
                    actions = [
                        {
                            "_index": f"bigdata-logs-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}",
                            "_source": entry
                        }
                        for entry in batch
                    ]
                    success, failed = helpers.bulk(es_client, actions, chunk_size=1000)
                    with self.stats_lock:
                        self.stats["processed"] += success
                        self.stats["failed"] += len(failed)
                        self.stats["batch_size"] += len(batch)

                    if failed:
                        logger.warning(f"部分日志发送到Elasticsearch失败: {len(failed)}")
                        # 重试失败的条目
                        for item in failed:
                            self.queue.put(item["_source"])

                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"批量发送到Elasticsearch失败: {str(e)}")
                    # 回退到队列
                    for entry in batch:
                        self.queue.put(entry)

            # 2. 发送关键日志到Kafka（错误级别以上）
            if kafka_producer:
                error_logs = [entry for entry in batch
                             if entry.get("level") in ["ERROR", "CRITICAL", "FATAL"]]

                for log in error_logs:
                    try:
                        kafka_producer.produce(
                            'bigdata-alerts',
                            key=log.get("trace.id", str(uuid.uuid4())),
                            value=json.dumps(log),
                            callback=self._kafka_delivery_callback
                        )
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"发送到Kafka失败: {str(e)}")

                kafka_producer.poll(0)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"批处理失败: {str(e)}")
            # 确保队列任务完成
            for _ in batch:
                self.queue.task_done()

    def _kafka_delivery_callback(self, err, msg):
        """Kafka消息发送回调"""
        if err:
            logger.error(f"Kafka消息发送失败: {str(err)}")

    def add_log(self, log_entry):
        """添加日志到处理队列，包含背压处理"""
        try:
            # 非阻塞方式添加，避免生产过快导致阻塞
            self.queue.put(log_entry, block=False)
            return True
        except queue.Full:
            # 队列已满，实施背压策略
            logger.warning("日志队列已满，实施背压策略")
            # 采样丢弃部分非关键日志
            if log_entry.get("level") in ["DEBUG", "INFO"]:
                if random.random() > 0.1:  # 90%的低级别日志被丢弃
                    return False

            # 尝试阻塞添加关键日志
            try:
                self.queue.put(log_entry, block=True, timeout=0.1)
                return True
            except queue.Full:
                logger.error("日志队列溢出，丢弃日志")
                return False

    def flush(self):
        """强制刷新队列中的所有日志"""
        self.queue.join()

# 初始化高级日志处理器
log_processor = AdvancedLogProcessor(worker_count=8)
log_processor.start()

# 分布式追踪辅助类
class DistributedContext:
    @staticmethod
    def create_context():
        """创建分布式上下文（包含追踪ID等）"""
        trace_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        span_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:16]  # 短版span_id
        return {
            "trace_id": trace_id,
            "span_id": span_id,
            "context_id": trace_id,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }

    @staticmethod
    def inject_context(log_extra, context):
        """将分布式上下文注入日志extra字段"""
        log_extra.update({
            "trace_id": context["trace_id"],
            "span_id": context["span_id"]
        })
        return log_extra

# 大数据处理日志装饰器
def log_processing_stage(pipeline_name, stage_name):
    """用于大数据处理阶段的日志装饰器"""
    def decorator(func):
        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            # 创建追踪上下文
            context = DistributedContext.create_context()

            # 记录开始日志
            start_time = time.time()
            batch_size = kwargs.get("batch_size", len(args[0]) if args else 0)

            logger.info(
                f"开始处理阶段",
                extra=DistributedContext.inject_context({
                    "pipeline": pipeline_name,
                    "stage": stage_name,
                    "record_count": batch_size,
                    "event_type": "stage_start",
                    "partition": kwargs.get("partition", 0),
                    "retry_count": kwargs.get("retry_count", 0)
                }, context)
            )

            try:
                # 执行实际处理逻辑
                with tracer.start_as_current_span(f"{pipeline_name}.{stage_name}") as span:
                    span.set_attribute("pipeline.name", pipeline_name)
                    span.set_attribute("stage.name", stage_name)
                    span.set_attribute("record.count", batch_size)

                    result = func(*args, **kwargs)

                    # 记录完成日志
                    processing_time = time.time() - start_time
                    successful_records = result.get("processed", batch_size)
                    failed_records = batch_size - successful_records
                    data_quality = result.get("quality", 1.0)

                    # 构建结构化日志条目
                    process_log = {
                        "@timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                        "level": "INFO",
                        "service": "bigdata-pipeline",
                        "message": f"阶段处理完成",
                        "trace.id": context["trace_id"],
                        "span.id": context["span_id"],
                        "pipeline": pipeline_name,
                        "stage": stage_name,
                        "record_count": batch_size,
                        "successful_records": successful_records,
                        "failed_records": failed_records,
                        "processing_time_ms": int(processing_time * 1000),
                        "data_quality": round(data_quality, 3),
                        "event_type": "stage_complete",
                        "throughput": round(successful_records / max(processing_time, 0.001), 2)
                    }

                    # 添加到日志处理器
                    log_processor.add_log(process_log)

                    # 如果有失败记录，记录错误日志
                    if failed_records > 0:
                        error_percentage = (failed_records / batch_size) * 100
                        error_log = {
                            "@timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                            "level": "ERROR",
                            "service": "bigdata-pipeline",
                            "message": f"处理阶段出现错误",
                            "trace.id": context["trace_id"],
                            "span.id": context["span_id"],
                            "pipeline": pipeline_name,
                            "stage": stage_name,
                            "failed_count": failed_records,
                            "error_percentage": round(error_percentage, 2),
                            "event_type": "stage_error",
                            "error_details": result.get("errors", [])
                        }
                        log_processor.add_log(error_log)

                    return result

            except Exception as e:
                # 异常日志，包含详细上下文
                processing_time = time.time() - start_time
                error_log = {
                    "@timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                    "level": "CRITICAL",
                    "service": "bigdata-pipeline",
                    "message": f"处理阶段失败: {str(e)}",
                    "trace.id": context["trace_id"],
                    "span.id": context["span_id"],
                    "pipeline": pipeline_name,
                    "stage": stage_name,
                    "record_count": batch_size,
                    "processing_time_ms": int(processing_time * 1000),
                    "event_type": "stage_failure",
                    "error": {
                        "type": type(e).__name__,
                        "message": str(e),
                        "stack_trace": traceback.format_exc()
                    }
                }
                log_processor.add_log(error_log)

                raise

        return wrapper

    return decorator

# 模拟大数据处理阶段
@log_processing_stage("customer_data_etl", "extract")
def extract_stage(data_source, batch_size, **kwargs):
    """模拟数据提取阶段"""
    # 模拟处理延迟
    processing_time = random.uniform(0.1, 2.0)
    time.sleep(processing_time)

    # 模拟处理结果
    success_rate = random.uniform(0.97, 1.0)
    successful_records = int(batch_size * success_rate)
    failed_records = batch_size - successful_records

    # 生成一些错误详情
    errors = []
    if failed_records > 0:
        error_types = ["schema_mismatch", "corrupt_data", "timeout"]
        for _ in range(min(5, failed_records)):  # 最多5条错误详情
            errors.append({
                "type": random.choice(error_types),
                "record_index": random.randint(0, batch_size - 1),
                "message": f"示例错误消息"
            })

    return {
        "success": True,
        "processed": successful_records,
        "failed": failed_records,
        "quality": random.uniform(0.9, 1.0),
        "errors": errors
    }

@log_processing_stage("customer_data_etl", "transform")
def transform_stage(data, batch_size, **kwargs):
    """模拟数据转换阶段"""
    # 模拟处理延迟
    processing_time = random.uniform(0.2, 3.0)
    time.sleep(processing_time)

    # 模拟处理结果
    success_rate = random.uniform(0.95, 1.0)
    successful_records = int(batch_size * success_rate)

    return {
        "success": True,
        "processed": successful_records,
        "quality": random.uniform(0.85, 0.98)
    }

@log_processing_stage("customer_data_etl", "load")
def load_stage(transformed_data, batch_size, **kwargs):
    """模拟数据加载阶段"""
    # 模拟处理延迟
    processing_time = random.uniform(0.15, 2.5)
    time.sleep(processing_time)

    # 模拟处理结果
    success_rate = random.uniform(0.98, 1.0)
    successful_records = int(batch_size * success_rate)

    return {
        "success": True,
        "processed": successful_records,
        "quality": random.uniform(0.95, 1.0)
    }

@log_processing_stage("daily_analytics", "aggregate")
def aggregate_stage(data, batch_size, **kwargs):
    """模拟数据分析聚合阶段"""
    # 模拟处理延迟
    processing_time = random.uniform(0.5, 5.0)
    time.sleep(processing_time)

    # 模拟处理结果
    success_rate = random.uniform(0.96, 1.0)
    successful_records = int(batch_size * success_rate)

    # 增加分析特定指标
    return {
        "success": True,
        "processed": successful_records,
        "quality": random.uniform(0.92, 1.0),
        "aggregation_metrics": {
            "avg_value": random.uniform(100, 1000),
            "record_groups": random.randint(100, 1000),
            "outliers_detected": random.randint(0, 50)
        }
    }

# ETL流水线执行类
class DataPipeline:
    def __init__(self, name, stages):
        self.name = name
        self.stages = stages
        self.metrics = {
            "executions": 0,
            "total_records": 0,
            "successful_records": 0,
            "total_time_ms": 0
        }

    def execute(self, initial_batch_size, **kwargs):
        """执行完整的数据处理流水线"""
        pipeline_start = time.time()
        self.metrics["executions"] += 1
        self.metrics["total_records"] += initial_batch_size

        try:
            # 创建流水线级别的上下文
            pipeline_context = DistributedContext.create_context()

            # 记录流水线开始
            logger.info(
                f"开始执行数据流水线",
                extra=DistributedContext.inject_context({
                    "pipeline": self.name,
                    "event_type": "pipeline_start",
                    "initial_batch_size": initial_batch_size,
                    "configuration": kwargs
                }, pipeline_context)
            )

            # 执行各阶段
            current_batch_size = initial_batch_size
            for stage_func in self.stages:
                result = stage_func(
                    data=None,  # 简化示例
                    batch_size=current_batch_size,
                    pipeline_context=pipeline_context,
                    **kwargs
                )
                current_batch_size = result["processed"]

            # 记录流水线完成
            pipeline_time = time.time() - pipeline_start
            self.metrics["successful_records"] += current_batch_size
            self.metrics["total_time_ms"] += int(pipeline_time * 1000)

            logger.info(
                f"数据流水线执行完成",
                extra=DistributedContext.inject_context({
                    "pipeline": self.name,
                    "event_type": "pipeline_complete",
                    "initial_batch_size": initial_batch_size,
                    "final_batch_size": current_batch_size,
                    "processing_time_ms": int(pipeline_time * 1000),
                    "metrics": self.metrics
                }, pipeline_context)
            )

            return {"success": True, "processed": current_batch_size}

        except Exception as e:
            # 记录流水线失败
            pipeline_time = time.time() - pipeline_start
            logger.error(
                f"数据流水线执行失败: {str(e)}",
                extra={
                    "pipeline": self.name,
                    "event_type": "pipeline_failure",
                    "initial_batch_size": initial_batch_size,
                    "processing_time_ms": int(pipeline_time * 1000),
                    "exception": traceback.format_exc()
                }
            )
            raise

# 创建并配置流水线
customer_etl_pipeline = DataPipeline(
    "customer_data_etl",
    [extract_stage, transform_stage, load_stage]
)

daily_analytics_pipeline = DataPipeline(
    "daily_analytics",
    [aggregate_stage]
)

# 主程序 - 模拟ETL流水线执行
print("启动大数据日志收集与处理示例...")
try:
    cycle_count = 0
    while True:
        cycle_count += 1
        print(f"执行周期 {cycle_count}")

        # 执行客户数据ETL流水线
        try:
            customer_etl_pipeline.execute(
                initial_batch_size=100000,
                partition=cycle_count % 10,
                priority="normal"
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"ETL流水线执行失败，但继续运行: {str(e)}")

        # 执行每日分析流水线（每3个周期执行一次）
        if cycle_count % 3 == 0:
            try:
                daily_analytics_pipeline.execute(
                    initial_batch_size=500000,
                    partition=0,
                    priority="high"
                )
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"分析流水线执行失败，但继续运行: {str(e)}")

        # 定期刷新日志
        log_processor.flush()

        # 等待下一轮
        time.sleep(10)

        # 为演示目的，限制执行次数
        if cycle_count >= 5:
            print("演示完成，退出循环")
            break

# 优雅退出处理
except KeyboardInterrupt:
    print("收到中断信号，正在停止...")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"发生意外错误: {str(e)}")
finally:
    print("正在清理资源...")
    # 确保所有日志都被处理
    log_processor.flush()
    # 停止日志处理器
    log_processor.stop()
    # 刷新Kafka生产者
    if kafka_producer:
        kafka_producer.flush()
    print("清理完成，程序退出")```

```

**高级Logstash配置示例**（用于大数据环境的日志收集和转换）：

```yaml

# 大数据环境高级Logstash配置 - logstash.conf
input {
  # Kafka输入优化配置
  kafka {
    bootstrap_servers => "kafka-broker1:9092,kafka-broker2:9092,kafka-broker3:9092"
    topics => ["bigdata-logs", "app-metrics", "service-logs"]
    group_id => "logstash-consumer-group"
    codec => json
    # 性能优化配置
    consumer_threads => 12
    fetch_max_bytes => 104857600
    max_partition_fetch_bytes => 20971520
    # 故障恢复与弹性配置
    auto_offset_reset => "latest"
    heartbeat_interval_ms => 3000
    session_timeout_ms => 30000
    # 批处理优化
    poll_timeout_ms => 5000
    decorate_events => true
    # 安全配置
    ssl_enabled => true
    sasl_mechanism => "PLAIN"
    sasl_username => "${LOGSTASH_KAFKA_USERNAME}"
    sasl_password => "${LOGSTASH_KAFKA_PASSWORD}"
  }

  # 多源文件输入配置
  file {
    id => "hadoop_logs"
    path => ["/var/log/hadoop/*/*.log", "/var/log/hadoop/*.log"]
    type => "hadoop"
    start_position => "beginning"
    sincedb_path => "/data/logstash/sincedb/hadoop"
    codec => multiline {
      pattern => "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2} [0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2},[0-9]{3}"
      negate => true
      what => "previous"
      max_lines => 500
      timeout_ms => 5000
    }
    close_older => 86400
    ignore_older => 604800
  }

  file {
    id => "spark_logs"
    path => ["/var/log/spark/*/*.log", "/var/log/spark/*.log"]
    type => "spark"
    start_position => "beginning"
    sincedb_path => "/data/logstash/sincedb/spark"
    codec => multiline {
      pattern => "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2} [0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2},[0-9]{3}"
      negate => true
      what => "previous"
      max_lines => 1000
      timeout_ms => 10000
    }
    close_older => 86400
  }

  # HTTP输入 - 用于接收API和微服务日志
  http {
    id => "http_api_logs"
    port => 8080
    codec => json
    # 安全配置
    user => "${LOGSTASH_HTTP_USERNAME}"
    password => "${LOGSTASH_HTTP_PASSWORD}"
  }
}

filter {
  # 数据类型转换 - 预处理优化
  if [level] or [log_level] or [severity] {
    mutate {
      rename => {
        "log_level" => "level"
        "severity" => "level"
      }
      lowercase => ["level"]
    }
  }

  # 结构化日志处理
  if [message] =~ "^{" {
    json {
      source => "message"
      target => "parsed_log"
      remove_field => ["message"]
      # 错误处理
      skip_on_invalid_json => true
      tag_on_failure => ["_jsonparsefailure"]
    }

    # 字段扁平化 - 简化查询
    ruby {
      code => 'if event.get("parsed_log") && event.get("parsed_log").is_a?(Hash)
                  event.get("parsed_log").each { |k, v|
                    unless event.include?(k) || ["@timestamp", "message", "tags"].include?(k)
                      event.set(k, v)
                    end
                  }
                end'
    }
  }

  # 多格式日志解析 - 使用条件过滤
  if [type] == "hadoop" {
    grok {
      match => {
        "message" => [
          "%{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:timestamp} %{LOGLEVEL:level} \[(?<component>[^\]]+)\] %{GREEDYDATA:log_message}",
          "%{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:timestamp} %{LOGLEVEL:level} %{GREEDYDATA:log_message}"
        ]
        "path" => [
          "/var/log/hadoop/%{GREEDYDATA:service}/%{GREEDYDATA:instance}.log",
          "/var/log/hadoop/%{GREEDYDATA:service}.log"
        ]
      }
      tag_on_failure => ["_hadoopgrokparsefailure"]
      overwrite => ["message"]
    }
  } else if [type] == "spark" {
    grok {
      match => {
        "message" => [
          "%{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:timestamp} %{LOGLEVEL:level} %{NUMBER:thread_id} %{GREEDYDATA:component} %{GREEDYDATA:log_message}",
          "%{TIMESTAMP_ISO8601:timestamp} %{LOGLEVEL:level} %{GREEDYDATA:log_message}"
        ]
        "path" => [
          "/var/log/spark/%{GREEDYDATA:application_id}/%{GREEDYDATA:component}.log",
          "/var/log/spark/%{GREEDYDATA:component}.log"
        ]
      }
      tag_on_failure => ["_sparkgrokparsefailure"]
      overwrite => ["message"]
    }
  }

  # 数据处理流水线特定字段提取
  if [pipeline] {
    mutate {
      add_field => {
        "source_type" => "data_pipeline"
      }
      # 转换数值字段为适当类型
      convert => {
        "record_count" => "integer"
        "failed_records" => "integer"
        "processing_time_ms" => "integer"
        "data_quality" => "float"
        "throughput" => "float"
      }
    }

    # 性能指标计算
    if [record_count] and [processing_time_ms] and [processing_time_ms] > 0 {
      ruby {
        code => 'event.set("records_per_second", event.get("record_count").to_f / (event.get("processing_time_ms").to_f / 1000.0))'
      }
    }
  }

  # 异常检测与标签化
  if [level] =~ "(error|critical|fatal)" {
    mutate {
      add_tag => ["_critical", "_requires_attention"]
      add_field => { "priority" => "high" }
    }
  }

  # 高基数字段处理 - 性能优化
  if [trace_id] {
    fingerprint {
      source => ["trace_id"]
      target => "trace_id_hash"
      method => "MD5"
    }
    mutate {
      # 保留trace_id的前8个字符用于部分匹配
      add_field => { "trace_id_prefix" => "%{trace_id}" }
    }
    ruby {
      code => 'if event.get("trace_id_prefix") && event.get("trace_id_prefix").length > 8
                  event.set("trace_id_prefix", event.get("trace_id_prefix")[0,8])
                end'
    }
  }

  # 时间戳规范化处理
  date {
    match => [
      "timestamp", "ISO8601",
      "@timestamp", "ISO8601",
      "event_time", "ISO8601",
      "time", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss,SSS",
      "time", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"
    ]
    target => "@timestamp"
    remove_field => ["timestamp", "event_time", "time"]
    tag_on_failure => ["_dateparsefailure"]
  }

  # 环境和集群信息增强
  mutate {
    add_field => {
      "environment" => "%{[env]}"
      "cluster" => "%{[kubernetes][cluster_name]}"
    }
    # 处理环境变量，确保字段存在
    if ![environment] {
      add_field => { "environment" => "production" }
    }
    if ![cluster] {
      add_field => { "cluster" => "data-lake-cluster" }
    }
    # 标准化环境名称
    gsub => [
      "environment", "^prod", "production",
      "environment", "^dev", "development",
      "environment", "^test", "testing"
    ]
  }

  # 地理位置和节点信息增强
  if [host] and [host] =~ "^data-node" {
    mutate {
      add_field => { "node_type" => "data_node" }
    }
  } else if [host] and [host] =~ "^master" {
    mutate {
      add_field => { "node_type" => "master_node" }
    }
  }
}

output {
  # 错误路由 - 将解析失败的日志单独存储
  if "_jsonparsefailure" in [tags] or "_grokparsefailure" in [tags] {
    elasticsearch {
      id => "failed_parsing_output"
      hosts => ["elasticsearch:9200"]
      index => "bigdata-logs-parse-failures-%{+YYYY.MM.dd}"
      workers => 2
      batch_size => 1000
      # 安全配置
      user => "${ELASTICSEARCH_USERNAME}"
      password => "${ELASTICSEARCH_PASSWORD}"
      ssl => true
    }
  }

  # 主要Elasticsearch输出 - 按环境和类型分片
  elasticsearch {
    id => "primary_elasticsearch_output"
    hosts => ["elasticsearch:9200"]
    # 动态索引命名
    index => "bigdata-logs-%{environment}-%{+YYYY.MM.dd}"
    # 高级优化配置
    template_overwrite => true
    # 性能优化
    workers => 10
    batch_size => 8000
    # 索引生命周期管理
    ilm_enabled => true
    ilm_policy => "bigdata-logs-policy"
    ilm_rollover_alias => "bigdata-logs-%{environment}"
    # 文档ID生成策略
    document_id => "%{[@metadata][fingerprint]}"
    # 安全配置
    user => "${ELASTICSEARCH_USERNAME}"
    password => "${ELASTICSEARCH_PASSWORD}"
    ssl => true
    # 故障恢复配置
    retry_on_conflict => 5
    sniffing => true
    sniffing_delay => 30s
    # 死信队列
    dead_letter_queue_enabled => true
    dead_letter_queue_path => "/data/logstash/dlq"
  }

  # 告警输出 - 多级告警路由
  if [priority] == "high" or [level] =~ "(error|critical|fatal)" {
    # 高优先级告警路由到专门的Kafka主题
    kafka {
      id => "high_priority_alerts"
      bootstrap_servers => "kafka:9092"
      topic_id => "bigdata-critical-alerts"
      codec => json
      # 消息可靠性配置
      acks => "all"
      retries => 10
      # 安全配置
      ssl_enabled => true
      sasl_mechanism => "PLAIN"
      sasl_username => "${LOGSTASH_KAFKA_USERNAME}"
      sasl_password => "${LOGSTASH_KAFKA_PASSWORD}"
    }

    # 同时发送到专用告警索引
    elasticsearch {
      id => "alerts_elasticsearch_output"
      hosts => ["elasticsearch:9200"]
      index => "bigdata-alerts-%{+YYYY.MM.dd}"
      workers => 4
      batch_size => 2000
      # 安全配置
      user => "${ELASTICSEARCH_USERNAME}"
      password => "${ELASTICSEARCH_PASSWORD}"
      ssl => true
    }
  }

  # 性能指标输出 - 分离存储用于时序分析
  if [source_type] == "data_pipeline" {
    elasticsearch {
      id => "metrics_elasticsearch_output"
      hosts => ["elasticsearch:9200"]
      index => "bigdata-metrics-%{+YYYY.MM.dd}"
      workers => 6
      batch_size => 5000
      # 专门的ILM策略用于时序数据
      ilm_enabled => true
      ilm_policy => "bigdata-metrics-policy"
      # 安全配置
      user => "${ELASTICSEARCH_USERNAME}"
      password => "${ELASTICSEARCH_PASSWORD}"
      ssl => true
    }
  }

  # 调试输出 - 仅在开发环境启用
  if [environment] == "development" {
    stdout {
      codec => rubydebug
    }
  }
}

```

**高级Kibana查询和可视化示例**：

```kql

# 1. 高级错误分析 - 按组件和错误类型分组
level:ERROR OR level:CRITICAL
| stats count() as error_count,
       values(error.type) as error_types,
       avg(processing_time_ms) as avg_processing_time
       by component, pipeline, stage
| sort error_count desc

# 2. 异常检测查询 - 识别异常延迟模式
pipeline:*
| where processing_time_ms > 0
| stats avg(processing_time_ms) as avg_latency,
       stddev(processing_time_ms) as stddev_latency,
       percentiles(processing_time_ms, 95) as p95_latency
       by pipeline, stage, kubernetes.pod_name
| eval threshold = avg_latency + (3 * stddev_latency)
| where p95_latency > threshold

# 3. 多维度数据质量分析
pipeline:*
| stats avg(data_quality) as avg_quality,
       min(data_quality) as min_quality,
       max(data_quality) as max_quality
       by pipeline, stage, environment, date_format(@timestamp, "yyyy-MM-dd")
| where avg_quality < 0.95
| sort min_quality asc

# 4. 资源使用关联分析
kubernetes.pod_name:*
| stats avg(cpu_usage_percent) as avg_cpu,
       max(memory_usage_bytes) as max_memory,
       avg(processing_time_ms) as avg_latency
       by kubernetes.pod_name, kubernetes.node_name
| where avg_cpu > 75 or max_memory > 4000000000

# 5. 分布式追踪分析
pipeline:* AND trace_id:*
| where @timestamp > now()-1h
| stats count() as event_count,
       min(@timestamp) as start_time,
       max(@timestamp) as end_time,
       values(event_type) as event_types
       by trace_id, pipeline
| eval duration_ms = (end_time - start_time) / 1000000
| sort duration_ms desc

# 6. 按时间段的错误率趋势 - 高级时间分析
level:ERROR
| timechart span=15m
       count() as error_count,
       count(if(level="ERROR", 1, null)) / count() * 100 as error_percentage
       by pipeline, environment
| where error_percentage > 5

```

  "analysis_config": {
    "bucket_span": "5m",
    "detectors": [
      {
        "function": "metric",
        "field_name": "processing_time_ms",
        "by_field_name": "pipeline",

      }

  },

    "time_field": "@timestamp"
  },

    "query": {
      "match": {
        "source_type": "data_pipeline"
      }
    }
  }
}
